400 data/text/birch_speech.inc:17 gText_Birch_MainSpeech
This world is widely inhabited by creatures known as POKéMON. / But this HOENN is a little different. Here, POKéMON are built, not raised. / Let me walk you through it. First, catch POKéMON. Lots of them. / Every one has perfect potential and comes with a competitive BATTLE SET already on it. / Wild POKéMON from every region live throughout HOENN. Some legendary discoveries are 1% wild encounters. / SWEET SCENT gives a 25% chance of an available local legendary. / Shared areas split that chance evenly. Badges and story requirements apply. / The REPEL SPRAY keeps the rest away while you travel. / Next, level them. The LEVELER raises your whole team to the level cap instantly, as often as you like. / The cap rises with each BADGE. There is no grinding. Ever. / BADGES also unlock CUT, SURF and the rest. Any POKéMON in your party that could learn one can use it. / The FLIGHT BEACON calls a ride the moment you can FLY. / Then build them. Every POKéMON CENTER has a TUTOR. Talk to them. / The TUTOR teaches any move that POKéMON can learn. / The TUTOR changes its NATURE. Its ABILITY you can change yourself, right from the party menu. / The TUTOR edits its EVs: 510 to spend, up to 252 per stat. Their effect scales with level. / The TUTOR shows the stat as you spend, so tune it. Don't guess. / Or skip all that and pick a BATTLE SET: moves, NATURE, held item and EVs in one choice. / Held items are free from the VENDOR in every POKéMON CENTER. / All of it is free. Change it anytime. / Then battle. Battles are mostly DOUBLES, and TRAINERS play to win. / When you pick a move, press L to see the foes' types. R shows the move. / You will lose sometimes. That's the point, not the end. / Save before a big battle. If you lose, choose RELOAD in the menu and retry. / Catch. Level. Build. Battle. Then build again, better. / Think of it as POKéMON CHAMPIONS, played across all of HOENN. / That's what I do here now: I help TRAINERS build. So, let's begin. / $
